# Not examined (all areas)

- [alpha-reduction-math] retarded-search.mjs (1,596 lines), domain-partition.mjs, partitioned-search.mjs, search.mjs, interval-search.mjs and validated-search.mjs beyond lines 100-160: the subdivision loops and verdict logic were not audited | container.mjs integrity/parsing policy and the byte-source loaders | The compiler's proven refit bound derivation (tools/compiler/compile.mjs beyond the EMRAT block) and the 0.0224 km geocentric figure itself; no real pack was compiled, so tier-b tests were not run | Numerical verification of the interval derivative formulas in aberration.mjs, deflection.mjs and frame-of-date.mjs against finite differences (read for form only) | The DEFLECTION-PROFILE.md ray-integration and second-order claims, and the ERFA eraLd fixture regeneration | Swiss Ephemeris's own TDB-TT handling and its exact aberration/deflection formulas (Swiss treated as a black-box instrument) | Shapiro delay magnitude (estimated from order-of-magnitude, not measured) | Horizons apparent RA/Dec (quantity 2) as an alternative mas-level arbiter; only quantity 31 and its legend were examined | Cross-runtime (browser) reproducibility evidence and instrument.mjs | Performance/cost of the reduction (only elapsed times of my own harnesses were recorded)
- [alpha-search-partition] The empirical apparent mode (search.mjs / interval-search.mjs) was audited by reading only; no fixture was run through runtime.search() beyond the repo's own angle-counterexamples suite (which passes). | The internals of the interval frame chain (frame-of-date.mjs frameMatrixInterval, ttCenturiesInterval), aberrateInterval and deflectInterval beyond their gate logic and calling conventions; assumed sound per the other auditors' areas. | The three tier-b holdout files (retarded, aberrated, of-date) were not re-run: 20 cases each at up to 76 s per case exceeds the compute cap; the committed *-RESULTS.md figures are cited instead. | The pack-versus-kernel bound and the compiler's fitting error (tools/compiler, tier-b pack-versus-kernel.nodetest.mjs) — outside this area. | The knobs sweep under docs/platform/evidence/precision-partition/knobs/ (cited via PARTITION-RESULTS, not reopened). | The browser runtime, the preview worker and cross-runtime byte-identity tests. | JPL Horizons was not queried: the search layer's claims are about the pack's polynomial, so a sky arbiter would not change any verdict here (0 of the ~30-request allowance used). | The domainProvedByCaller soundness argument in deflection.mjs:580-612 was read, not exercised beyond the shipped tests. | Wall-clock cost of seriesBounds being recomputed (with Float64Array allocation) on every stateEnclosure call; likely a 2-3x wall inefficiency, not measured.
- [angles-houses-aspects] JPL Horizons was not queried: it computes no ascendant or houses, so ERFA (IAU 2006/2000A) served as the independent arbiter for angles instead | Actual IERS UT1-UTC values for any date (only the 0.9 s bound was used); both engine and Swiss were run with UTC as UT1 | Koch, Regiomontanus, Campanus and the other Swiss systems numerically (nothing in the stack implements them, so only their existence and polar behaviour were measured) | Sidereal (ayanamsa) houses, topocentric planet positions and Gauquelin sectors | The precision alpha (examples/precision-alpha) - it has no house or aspect code | Dates outside 1800-2200 and latitudes between 66.6 and 89.9 other than the six sweep latitudes | src/lib/compat.ts content (sign-pair prose; no computation to audit beyond pairSlug) | Aspect-pattern detection numerics beyond code reading (tests exist in src/lib/engine/aspect-patterns.test.ts); the TS modules with extensionless imports (declination.ts, composite.ts) could not be executed directly and were verified by verbatim formula ports and their committed tests | Synastry and composite behaviour beyond code reading and the wrap arithmetic (no Swiss counterpart) | MCP tool surface (src/mcp/*.ts) and how it exposes house/aspect results | Returns, progressions and transit angles (other auditors' areas) except the station-speed probe | The frozen swiss-node-polar fixture bytes were not re-hashed; docs/engine-validation/swiss-node-polar/README.md was read and its conventions cross-checked against the live Swiss behaviour measured here
